<script>
function sr_menus_setupMainNav_button_inline () {
    if (sr_detect_operaMini || !("classList" in document.createElement("_"))) {
	return false;
    }
    var nav_trigger = document.getElementById('nav_trigger');
    if(!nav_trigger || nav_trigger.triggered) {
	return false;
    }
    nav_trigger.triggered = true;
    var nav = document.getElementById('nav');
    var nav_trigger_a = nav_trigger.querySelector('a');
    if (nav_trigger_a) {
	nav_trigger_a.setAttribute('href','javascript:void(0)');
	nav_trigger.onclick = function (event) {
	    nav.classList.toggle('open');
	    var is_open = nav.classList.contains('open');
	    if (is_open) {
		nav_trigger.classList.add('open');
	    }
	    else {
		nav_trigger.classList.remove('open');
	    }
	    event.preventDefault();
	    try { sr_record_analytics_event('MainNavButtonClick_inline',sr_record_directory(),sr_record_page());}
	    catch(err) {}
	};
    }
    return true;
}
sr_menus_setupMainNav_button_inline();

            <script type="text/javascript" class="allowed">

// Update the shot chart
function update_shot_chart(team_id) {

  var q_elem = document.getElementById('quarter-' + team_id);
  var p_elem = document.getElementById('player-'  + team_id);
  var s_elem = document.getElementById('shots-'   + team_id);

  var quarter = q_elem.options[q_elem.selectedIndex].value;
  var player  = p_elem.options[p_elem.selectedIndex].value;

  for (var i = 1; i < s_elem.childNodes.length; i++) {

    var c_elem = s_elem.childNodes[i];

    if (c_elem.nodeName.toLowerCase() !== 'div') { continue; }

    if ((c_elem.className.indexOf('p-' + player) === -1)
         || (c_elem.className.indexOf('q-' + quarter) === -1)) {

      c_elem.style.visibility = "hidden";
    }
    else {
      c_elem.style.visibility = "visible";
    }
  }
}

// Reset the shot chart
function reset_shot_chart(team_id) {

  var q_elem = document.getElementById('quarter-' + team_id);
  var p_elem = document.getElementById('player-'  + team_id);
  var s_elem = document.getElementById('shots-'   + team_id);

  for (var i = 0; i < s_elem.childNodes.length; i++) {

    var c_elem = s_elem.childNodes[i];

    if (c_elem.nodeName.toLowerCase() !== 'div') { continue; }

    c_elem.style.visibility = "visible";
  }

  q_elem.selectedIndex = 0;
  p_elem.selectedIndex = 0;
}
</script>
